working assessment

Hypothesis, not an established finding: Assess how Home Slice Pizza (1415 S Congress Ave, Austin, TX 78704) ranks across 1,249 shared search origins in a 10-mile radius (0.5-mile spacing, top-30 organic depth, search_this_area=false) for four core search themes: pizza restaurant, new york style pizza, pizza by the slice, and pizza takeout.

important limitations

Search rankings measure organic Google Maps visibility at sampled coordinates; they do not measure walk-in traffic, online ordering conversions, or revenue.

Two queries (new york style pizza and pizza takeout) each had 1 origin out of 1,249 where the provider returned fewer than 30 organic listings after retry; those 2 observations are marked as incomplete absence rather than confirmed top-30 absence.

study objectives and measurement boundaries

objectives

Measure top-3 and top-10 organic Maps visibility across 1,249 shared origins (10-mile radius, 0.5-mile grid spacing) for four distinct customer search themes.

Compare category discovery (pizza restaurant) against style-specific (new york style pizza), format-specific (pizza by the slice), and fulfillment-specific (pizza takeout) searches.

Distinguish genuine visibility drop-offs from Austin sister-location handoffs (More Home Slice at 1421 S Congress Ave and North Loop at 501 E 53rd St).

Identify ranks 4-10 strike zones where targeted website and profile clarity can be evaluated.

evidence boundaries

Measured means found or not returned. Empty, error, and unmeasured origins are excluded. Top-3 and top-10 shares are observed appearances divided by valid measured origins; they are not area or market coverage. A requested depth or returned count alone does not prove absence at a cutoff. Near misses are exactly ranks 4–10 inclusive. Results describe supplied origins and timestamps only; no sampled boundary proves total absence.

Business origin: Home Slice Pizza (user supplied). Coordinates: 30.2492286, -97.7495328.

User-supplied evidence; this renderer does not independently authenticate source labels or origin records. Acquisition verification requires a separate review of the underlying evidence.

reading the findings

Each dot represents a place someone could search from. Its number is the listing position returned for that search, not a customer count. Different searches can reveal different discovery gaps.

For 'pizza restaurant', the listing appeared among the first three results at 21 of 1249 measured locations, and among the first ten at 261.

For 'new york style pizza', the listing appeared among the first three results at 549 of 1249 measured locations, and among the first ten at 1023.

For 'pizza by the slice', the listing appeared among the first three results at 470 of 1249 measured locations, and among the first ten at 952.

For 'pizza takeout', the listing appeared among the first three results at 6 of 1249 measured locations, and among the first ten at 222.

These observations do not establish why rankings differ, how many people searched, or whether a change will generate sales.

why these searches matter

SEARCH 01

pizza restaurant

Find a local pizza restaurant for dining in or ordering a whole pie.

Primary GBP category discovery across Austin dining neighborhoods.

Evidence boundaries

Broad category searches include dine-in, slice shops, and chain pizzerias; national volume is context only.

SEARCH 02

new york style pizza

Find authentic hand-tossed thin-crust New York-style pizza in Austin.

Core culinary identity explicitly stated on the homepage and dominant in customer place topics.

Evidence boundaries

Searchers may type ny pizza or new york style pizza; one representative phrase is selected for the style theme.

SEARCH 03

pizza by the slice

Grab casual individual pizza slices without ordering a full whole pie.

Signature walk-in slice format highlighted on both the website and Google Business Profile.

Evidence boundaries

Includes both dedicated counter-service slice windows (including More Home Slice next door) and full-service pizzerias.

SEARCH 04

pizza takeout

Order whole pies or slices for pickup and takeaway.

Explicit secondary GBP category (Pizza Takeout) and carry-out fulfillment offering on the website.

Evidence boundaries

Takeout intent is more distance-sensitive than destination dining.

web companion lens 03

territory campaigns & action briefs

Home Slice Pizza South Congress (1415 S Congress Ave) operates alongside its dedicated walk-up slice and takeout sister counter More Home Slice (1421 S Congress Ave) and its North Loop location (501 E 53rd St). The 10-mile open-search study shows how specialty style authority (new york style pizza) and slice discovery (pizza by the slice) extend farther across Austin than general category or takeout searches. Public ranking observations identify where sister profiles and neighborhood rivals trade places; they do not establish order volume, margin, or ranking causation.

pizza restaurant / South Congress & Central Austin

anchor south congress category discovery on the dedicated location page

Observed: The verified Google Business Profile links directly to the South Congress location page, which provides street address, hours, parking instructions, and menu links.

Recommended action: Keep the South Congress GBP linked to its dedicated location page and verify structured restaurant metadata (hours, menu URL, neighborhood parking) rather than redirecting to the multi-city homepage.

Verification check: Maintained top-three category visibility in South River City, Travis Heights, Bouldin Creek, and Downtown alongside stable dine-in volume.

new york style pizza / Greater Austin specialty reach

reinforce ny-style culinary copy on the linked south congress page

Observed: The homepage highlights authentic NY-style pizza, while the linked South Congress location page focuses primarily on hours, address, and parking.

Recommended action: Add a concise, factual summary of the South Congress menu (hand-tossed NY-style whole pies, slices, calzones, and Italian subs) directly onto the South Congress location page.

Verification check: Sustained top-three specialty reach across South, Central, East, and West Austin origins.

pizza by the slice / South Congress walk-in & slice corridor

coordinate dine-in and walk-up slice window messaging on south congress

Observed: Both 1415 S Congress Ave and 1421 S Congress Ave compete in local results for slice searches, giving the brand dual visibility on South Congress.

Recommended action: Keep both verified profiles distinct and accurate, and ensure the South Congress location page clearly directs quick slice and carry-out guests to 1421 S Congress Ave next door.

Verification check: Reduced walk-up confusion and sustained top-three visibility for pizza by the slice across Central and South Austin.

pizza takeout / South & Central Austin carry-out catchment

highlight online ordering and dedicated pickup parking within the 3-mile carry-out ring

Observed: Takeout visibility drops off faster with distance than NY-style specialty searches, leaving a near-top-three ring across East Austin, Zilker, and South Lamar.

Recommended action: Surface direct online carry-out ordering and dedicated pickup parking details prominently on the South Congress location page and GBP order links.

Verification check: Increased completed online pickup orders and stronger top-three conversion in the 1.5-to-3.5-mile near-miss ring.

prioritized territory campaign sequence

Ready for page review / South Austin, Zilker & East Cesar Chavez · carry-out catchment

strengthen carry-out conversion in the 1.5-to-3.5-mile ring

Goal: Convert near-top-three takeout searchers within realistic drive time into direct online pickup orders.

Evidence: Home Slice lists Pizza Takeout as a verified GBP category and offers online ordering, while the South Congress page notes additional parking in the church lot off Elizabeth Street. Takeout rankings transition from top-three near 1415 S Congress to ranks 4-10 across surrounding 78704/78702 neighborhoods.

Verification gate: Confirm current online ordering URLs and pickup parking rules with South Congress store operations before editing page copy.

Copy enrichment candidate / Central & South Austin · specialty style discovery

align the south congress landing page with ny-style specialty searches

Goal: Support Home Slice's strongest regional discovery theme on the exact URL linked from Google Maps.

Evidence: The root homepage describes authentic NY-style pizza by the slice or pie, and GBP place topics show 452 mentions of ny style pizza, but the linked South Congress page has minimal descriptive text beyond hours and address.

Verification gate: Verify all menu items and dietary/ordering notes against the live South Congress menu.

Multi-profile coordination / South Congress corridor · slice & walk-up discovery

coordinate 1415 s congress (dine-in) and 1421 s congress (more home slice)

Goal: Help walk-in slice and carry-out customers choose the right storefront while preserving both verified profiles.

Evidence: Both Home Slice Pizza (1415 S Congress Ave) and More Home Slice Pizza (1421 S Congress Ave) appear in Austin Maps results for pizza by the slice and pizza takeout.

Verification gate: Keep both profiles strictly compliant with Google Business Profile guidelines for distinct storefronts.

Portfolio territory boundary / North Loop, Hyde Park & North Austin · sister territory

use north loop (501 e 53rd st) for north austin campaigns

Goal: Avoid wasting South Congress campaign effort in North Austin neighborhoods already served by the North Loop location.

Evidence: The 10-mile radius centered on South Congress extends north of UT Austin and Koenig Lane, where Home Slice's North Loop location (501 E 53rd St) is the closer storefront.

Verification gate: Confirm North Loop store capacity and operating hours before launching North Austin promotions.

recommended next steps

what to do next

Inspect the 216 near-miss origins (ranks 4-10) on pizza takeout (q4) in the Companion Campaigns view and compare the 1415 S Congress Ave profile against More Home Slice (1421 S Congress Ave).

Review the 240 near-miss origins on pizza restaurant (q1), 474 on new york style pizza (q2), and 482 on pizza by the slice (q3) along South Lamar, Zilker, East Austin, and Mueller.

Open the Keyword Stuffing tab in the Companion to inspect the 79 source-reviewed competitor titles and their 0-5 evidence confidence scores.

where additional measurement would help

Planning review uses a 0.75 km spacing preference, not a universal SEO threshold. These are recommendations, not additional measurements.

Multi-perspective strategy: The initial run dips a toe in the water with a high-resolution neighborhood baseline. Do not be cheap with DataForSEO tokens. A single baseline is step one; almost every business needs an outward enhancement, a regional zoom-out, or both to see the complete competitive picture across multiple zoom levels.

Directional expansion opportunity: pizza restaurant, new york style pizza, pizza by the slice, pizza takeout show competitive near-miss positions reaching the boundary. The next strategic move is expanding outward at the same high spatial resolution in those active directions using enhance to map adjacent neighborhood conversion opportunities.

pizza restaurant, new york style pizza, pizza by the slice, pizza takeout: maximum adjacent spacing 0.81 km; Top-ten visibility reaches this boundary. Consider a small outward probe only where populated territory, customer access or verified service scope makes it useful; inspect sharp neighboring rank transitions.

South, East, West: Most themes support investigating this direction. Subject to relevant geography, pitch a small shared outward band for all themes; an NR theme is a comparison/control, not an automatic veto. Price a bounded first band and reassess, rather than repeatedly extending.

Preserve this dated baseline and reuse compatible recent observations. To expand coverage, say enhance to your agent to estimate incremental collection cost and generate an updated report within your approved budget.

How the findings were checked

Home Slice Pizza operates three distinct Austin Google Business Profiles: 1415 S Congress Ave (CID 12299280573550492680, primary), More Home Slice at 1421 S Congress Ave (CID 8169083705981921284), and 501 E 53rd St (CID 5514358989167054199).

All 4,996 coordinate-query tasks were collected on 2026-10-19 via DataForSEO Google Maps Standard Queue with depth=30, zoom=13, and search_this_area=false ($2.9976 total scan spend).

what remains unverified

Search rankings measure organic Google Maps visibility at sampled coordinates; they do not measure walk-in traffic, online ordering conversions, or revenue.

Two queries (new york style pizza and pizza takeout) each had 1 origin out of 1,249 where the provider returned fewer than 30 organic listings after retry; those 2 observations are marked as incomplete absence rather than confirmed top-30 absence.
